## Idea

We introduce the
{{#concept "multiplication" Disambiguation="integers" Agda=mul-ℤ}} of integers
and derive its basic properties with respect to `succ-ℤ`, `pred-ℤ`, `neg-ℤ` and
`add-ℤ`.

## Definitions

<pre class="Agda"><a id="commutative-mul-ℤ"></a><a id="12689" href="elementary-number-theory.multiplication-integers.html#12689" class="Function">commutative-mul-ℤ</a> <a id="12707" class="Symbol">:</a>

### The multiplication of the negatives of two integers is equal to their multiplication

<a id="14093" href="elementary-number-theory.multiplication-integers.html#14021" class="Function">double-negative-law-mul-ℤ</a> <a id="14119" href="elementary-number-theory.multiplication-integers.html#14119" class="Bound">k</a> <a id="14121" href="elementary-number-theory.multiplication-integers.html#14121" class="Bound">l</a> <a id="14123" class="Symbol">=</a>
